You are reviewing one ticket in this repository (La Hoja Boricua, a vanilla JS PWA in site/ on Cloudflare Pages, Pages Functions in functions/, static pages generated by tools/build_*.py). Review only; do not edit files. Do not load or run any skills. The worktree is C:/LHB/lhb-marca108-wt (branch marca-108, cut from branch marca at b6afeeb, which carries LHB-107: Fraunces as the only display face and the type tokens). TIER 2, round 1.

Ticket LHB-108 (M8-2): one lockup and the tagline Hecho en Borinquen, on the app and every static page.
Pinned decisions:
- One lockup: the leaf mark (icons/icon.svg, decorative, alt="") plus the wordmark "La Hoja Boricua" in Fraunces plus the tagline "Hecho en Borinquen". Horizontal in headers; stacked in the intro (mark above the name).
- The tagline is part of the brand and is not translated: the English pages and the English app show it in Spanish, marked lang="es". The i18n keys ix.brandSub and intro.tagline are removed; the intro reads brand.tagline, whose value is the same in es and en. The intro's second slogan (Descubre el tabaco de Puerto Rico / Discover the tobacco of Puerto Rico) is gone.
- Static pages (lugares, artesanos, cigarros, guias, their English pages under en/), 404, privacy and the /u/<handle> passport page carry the same header markup as the app; their stylesheets (lugares/l.css, u.css) get the app's brand rules. Generated pages are regenerated by the generators, never hand-edited; tools/schema.py moves the English pages' relative paths one level deeper (href and now the logo src).
- Logo path: relative on generated pages and privacy (like every other link there), absolute (/icons/icon.svg) on 404 (served at any path) and on /u/ pages.
Contract impact: none to data or APIs. The /api/u/<handle> response is byte-identical; the /u/<handle> page differs only by the lockup (180 bytes), and its pinned hash in the LHB-74 test is updated.

Tests: (1) every HTML page under site/ (273) and the /u/ function carry exactly one lockup and no other brand link; (2) the tagline keys are gone, brand.tagline is the same in both languages, no other slogan remains, and the intro uses the stacked lockup. Both fail on marca (evidence/lhb-108-red.txt). Older tests updated on purpose: LHB-88 (the static brand link is the lockup and its logo is decorative), LHB-32 (the tagline may stay in Spanish on English pages), LHB-29 (the tagline is brand text on index.html), LHB-72 (the intro has 7 intro.* keys, not 8), LHB-74 (the /u/ page pin), the i18n sample now uses ix.footLug.
Full suite: evidence/lhb-108-tests.txt (413 of 414 pass, 1 skipped).
The diff below covers the sources (site/*.js, site/*.html, the stylesheets, tools, functions, test) and two generated samples (site/lugares/index.html, site/en/lugares/612-c-lounge-arecibo.html). The other 271 generated pages differ only in the same header line; read any of them in the worktree.

What to look for: a page or surface that still shows another wordmark or slogan (the share image drawn in hoja.js, the manifest, JSON-LD, the 404, the credits or about screens, llms.txt), a broken relative path (English pages at depth 2, guides, privacy), accessibility of the brand link (its name should read as the visible text; the logo must stay decorative), lang marking of the Spanish tagline, the header at 320 px wide (the lockup plus four buttons) and on the static pages at phone width, and anything in the tests that could pass while a page lacks the lockup.

Answer with findings ranked P1 (blocking) to P3, each with file and symbol, then VERDICT: PASS or VERDICT: BLOCK as the last line.

 <h1>¿Qué artesanos de cigarros hay en cada pueblo de Puerto Rico?</h1>
 <p class="answer">La Hoja Boricua tiene 32 artesanos de cigarros. 26 tienen pueblo o zona en la guía, en 19 pueblos y zonas: Adjuntas (1), Aguas Buenas (2), Área metro (1), Caguas (4), Cidra (2), Coamo (1), Humacao (1), Isabela (1), Juana Díaz (1), Lares (1), Mayagüez (1), Ponce (1), Río Grande (2), San Juan (1), Santa Isabel (2), Toa Alta (1), Toa Baja (1), Trujillo Alto (1) y Villalba (1). De 6 artesanos la guía no indica el pueblo.</p>

 <main>
 <h1>¿Qué es un cigarro hecho en Puerto Rico?</h1>
 <p class="answer">En La Hoja Boricua, un cigarro hecho en Puerto Rico es uno torcido en la isla. 2 de los 32 artesanos de la guía tienen una fuente que lo confirma: <a href="../artesanos/don-chamorro-ponce.html">Don Chamorro</a> y <a href="../artesanos/kikes-cigars-villalba.html">Don Kike Cigars (Kike&#x27;s Cigars)</a>. Para los demás, dónde se tuercen sus cigarros todavía no está verificado.</p>

+// en* da inglés, todo lo demás español; la escogida se recuerda en localStorage (lhb_lang)
+function pick(nav,saved){if(saved==='es'||saved==='en')return saved;return /^en/i.test(String(nav||''))?'en':'es'}
+let saved=null;try{saved=w.localStorage.getItem('lhb_lang')}catch(e){}
+const lang=ENABLED?pick(w.navigator&&w.navigator.language,saved):'es';
+if(w.document)w.document.documentElement.lang=lang==='en'?'en':'es-PR';
+// t('clave',{var}) llena {var}; si falta en inglés usa el español; si falta en los dos, el respaldo o la clave
+function t(k,v,fb){let s=T[lang][k];if(s==null)s=T.es[k];if(s==null)return fb!=null?fb:k;return v?s.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g,(m,n)=>n in v?String(v[n]):m):s}
+function set(l){try{w.localStorage.setItem('lhb_lang',l)}catch(e){}w.location.reload()}
+/* LHB-29: index.html trae el español; en inglés se cambia cada [data-i18n] (su HTML) y cada [data-i18n-attr] ("atributo:clave") */
+function apply(d){if(lang==='es')return;d.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el=>{el.innerHTML=t(el.getAttribute('data-i18n'))});d.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-attr]').forEach(el=>{for(const p of el.getAttribute('data-i18n-attr').split(';')){const i=p.indexOf(':');if(i>0)el.setAttribute(p.slice(0,i),t(p.slice(i+1)))}})}
+function wire(){apply(w.document);const b=w.document.getElementById('langSwitch');if(!b)return;if(!ENABLED){b.hidden=true;return}b.hidden=false;const nm=lang==='en'?'Español':'English';b.innerHTML='<span class="ls-long">'+nm+'</span><span class="ls-short" aria-hidden="true">'+(lang==='en'?'ES':'EN')+'</span>';b.setAttribute('aria-label',nm);b.lang=lang==='en'?'es':'en';b.onclick=()=>set(lang==='en'?'es':'en')}
+if(w.document){if(w.document.readyState==='loading')w.document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',wire);else wire()}
+w.LHB_I18N={t,lang,set,pick,ENABLED,T,apply};
+})(window);

 def en_head(head):
     # la cabecera de una página en /en/<sección>/: en inglés y un nivel más abajo
     out = head.replace('<html lang="es-PR">', '<html lang="en">').replace('href="l.css"', 'href="../lugares/l.css"')
-    out = out.replace('href="../', 'href="../../').replace('>Abrir en la app<', '>Open in the app<')
+    out = out.replace('href="../', 'href="../../').replace('src="../icons/', 'src="../../icons/').replace('>Abrir en la app<', '>Open in the app<')
     assert out.count('href="../../') >= 2 and 'lang="en"' in out, 'en_head: la cabecera no tiene la forma esperada'
     return out
